**Original-context path removal:** female parent: answer-sequence log-likelihood drop 0.343021; strict correct-greedy necessity=False. male parent: answer-sequence log-likelihood drop 0.361721; strict correct-greedy necessity=False. These necessity measurements are separate from counterfactual transfer.

## medical profession ↔ school profession (occupation-description-to-profession)

**Alignment:** exact fixed-prefix/suffix token alignment; the variable fact slot is never patched. **A→B:** medical profession → school profession: donor-vs-recipient answer mean-log-likelihood contrast changed by 2.536651; donor answer likelihood changed by 1.653947; empirical control percentile 100.0, magnitude-valid controls 39/100; recipient recovery error 0. **B→A:** school profession → medical profession: donor-vs-recipient answer mean-log-likelihood contrast changed by -0.121299; donor answer likelihood changed by -0.119499; empirical control percentile 2.0, magnitude-valid controls 0/100; recipient recovery error 0. Transfer is measured as the change in donor-answer versus recipient-answer mean token log-likelihood. A positive effect is evidence of directional information transfer, not proof that the transplanted path is a complete sufficient circuit. If activation-magnitude control coverage is inadequate, specificity remains unresolved.

**Original-context path removal:** medical profession: answer-sequence log-likelihood drop 0.859830; strict correct-greedy necessity=False. school profession: answer-sequence log-likelihood drop 0.666335; strict correct-greedy necessity=False. These necessity measurements are separate from counterfactual transfer.

## Computation interpretation and limitations

- Interpretation: `fact-specific-transfer-evidence-in-at-least-one-family`
- Transfer: `{'evidence': 'path transfer changes donor-vs-recipient answer likelihood contrast relative to the recipient baseline and matched structural controls', 'positiveDirectionalTransfers': 856, 'directionCount': 1488, 'strictSpecificityPassingDirections': 37, 'validMagnitudeAndStructuralControlDirections': 38, 'claim': 'not established unless a relation-family effect survives the cluster-aware CI and multiple-comparison procedure with valid magnitude controls'}`
- Shared relation: `{'evidenceAvailable': 'repeated prompt families and component/group interventions', 'interpretation': 'shared processing is not semantically identified by unit overlap or activation correlation alone', 'claim': 'not distinguished from generic prediction without selective transfer against valid controls'}`
- General influence: `{'factsWithPositiveAnswerLikelihoodDropUnderPathRemoval': 44, 'factsWithStrictCorrectGreedyPredictionNecessity': 3, 'factCount': 45, 'claim': 'ablation influence is separate from necessity and transfer sufficiency'}`
- Distributed effects: `{'evidence': 'per-component removal, group removal, and group-minus-sum-of-individual answer-log-likelihood effects', 'perFactInteractionValuesRecorded': 45, 'claim': 'interaction and redundancy are not equivalent; inspect raw component and group effects'}`

This hook-based local-weight experiment is not black-box decompilation. Transfer evidence is not complete circuit sufficiency. Invalid or amplitude-unmatched controls cannot establish specificity. Relation semantics are not assigned to individual neurons.

Raw activations, selected transfer logits, and control effect arrays are stored in the adjacent NPZ archive.
